Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the operators framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 669. The resulting operator achieves bound O(230/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on operators structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 669. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 230. QED.

Theorem 221.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any operators module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 155. The projective resolution has length at most 706 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^706(S, F) is trivial. QED.
